Like I was on another level with the jewelry work, and I could take any custom order and make it into something nobody else around here could really match.\n\nDoctor: During those times when you slept only a few hours, did you find yourself taking on a lot more projects or working much more than usual?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I did. I’d start a lot at once—lining up new commissions, messaging old customers, planning a website, sketching out this whole Appalachian-style collection. I don’t think I noticed that as much… maybe I was a little more keyed up, but talking more? I don’t know.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy times, did you feel unusually euphoric or irritable?\n\nPatient: Yeah… after the show I felt really lit up, like bigger than myself, I guess. But I’d also get irritated fast if someone told me to keep normal hours or slow down.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy times, did they ever get so severe that you had to be hospitalized?\n\nPatient: No… I don’t think it ever got to that point. At least, I wasn’t hospitalized for it.\n\nDoctor: Did those high-energy periods usually last at least four days in a row?\n\nPatient: Yeah… it was more than four days. The worst stretch started right after that jewelry show award, and it kind of ran on for… about two weeks, I think.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy periods, did you do things that were unusually risky or impulsive for you?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure. I mean… I probably made some quick decisions, but I don’t know if I’d call them risky. Nothing I really want to get into.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period lasting at least two weeks when you felt depressed most of the day nearly every day?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I guess so. It’s been more like a month now, this low, guilty feeling just kind of hanging over me most days.\n\nDoctor: During this past month, have you lost interest or pleasure in things you usually enjoy?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… maybe a little. I haven’t really been paying attention to what I enjoy lately.\n\nDoctor: During this past month, have you had trouble concentrating nearly every day?\n\nPatient: Yeah… some. At work I’ll just kind of stare at a repair ticket, or a tray of sapphires, and I can’t keep the measurements in my head long enough to figure out what I’m supposed to do next.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
